\displaystyle\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\gtrsim|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\geq|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(2)

Case of
r_{0}^{4}\leq|\beta_{k}|\leq r_{0}^{6}
.
Since
|\beta_{k}|\geq 1
, it renders
r_{0}\geq 1
. Utilizing Lemma
2.3
, we obtain the estimate of
\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(\frac{r_{0}}{2},\infty)}^{2}
as follows

\displaystyle\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\gtrsim\|r\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\geq\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{4}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(\frac{r_{0}}{2},\infty)}^{2}.

We continue to estimate
\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,\frac{r_{0}}{2})}^{2}
. Choose
r_{-}\in(\frac{r_{0}}{2}-\frac{1}{2r_{0}},\frac{r_{0}}{2})
such that the following inequality holds

(2.34)

\displaystyle\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{-})|^{2}}{r_{-}^{2}}\leq 2r_{0}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Observing the basic equality

\displaystyle\Im\langle F,\frac{w\chi_{(0,r_{-})}}{r^{2}}\rangle=

\displaystyle\Im\langle-[\partial_{r}^{2}w-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})w]+i\beta_{k}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\lambda)w,\frac{w\chi_{(0,r_{-})}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Im\langle-\partial_{r}^{2}w+i\beta_{k}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\lambda)w,\frac{w\chi_{(0,r_{-})}}{r^{2}}\rangle,

we deduce

\displaystyle|\beta_{k}|\langle(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\lambda)w,\frac{w\chi_{(0,r_{-})}}{r^{2}}\rangle\leq\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{-})w(r_{-})|}{|r_{-}|^{2}}.

It follows from the fact that
\lambda=\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}
and
r_{-}\in(\frac{r_{0}}{2}-\frac{1}{r_{0}},\frac{2r_{0}}{2})

\displaystyle\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{-})w(r_{-})|}{|r_{-}|^{2}}\geq|\beta_{k}|\langle(\frac{4}{r_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}})w,\frac{w\chi_{(0,r_{-})}}{r^{2}}\rangle\geq\frac{|\beta_{k}|}{r_{0}^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,r_{-})}^{2}.

Therefore,
\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}
can be bounded by

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,r_{-})}^{2}+\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(r_{-},\frac{r_{0}}{2})}^{2}+\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(\frac{r_{0}}{2},\infty)}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{|\beta_{k}|}(\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{-})w(r_{-})|}{|r_{-}|^{2}})+\frac{1}{r_{0}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}.

Together with (
2.34
), Lemma
A.2
, this yields

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{|\beta_{k}|}(\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+r_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}})+\frac{1}{r_{0}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{|\beta_{k}|}(\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+r_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\frac{w}{r})^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}})+\frac{1}{r_{0}}\|(\frac{w}{r})^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}.

By Lemma
2.3
we obtain

\displaystyle\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\frac{w}{r})^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}},

which implies

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{|\beta_{k}|}(\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+r_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\frac{w}{r})^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}})+\frac{1}{r_{0}}\|(\frac{w}{r})^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{|\beta_{k}|}(\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+r_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{5}{4}})+\frac{1}{r_{0}}\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}.

Then we conclude

\displaystyle\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\gtrsim\min\{\frac{|\beta_{k}|}{r_{0}^{2}},(\frac{|\beta_{k}|}{r_{0}^{\frac{5}{2}}})^{\frac{4}{3}},r_{0}^{2}\}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

With
r_{0}^{4}\leq|\beta_{k}|\leq r_{0}^{6}(\Rightarrow\frac{|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{4}{3}}}{r_{0}^{\frac{10}{3}}}\geq\frac{|\beta_{k}|}{r_{0}^{2}}\geq r_{0}^{2})
, we deduce

(2.35)

\displaystyle\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\gtrsim r_{0}^{2}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\geq|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(3)

Case of
|\beta_{k}|\geq r_{0}^{6}
.
Choose
r_{-}\in(r_{0}-\delta,r_{0}-\frac{\delta}{2})
and
r_{+}\in(r_{0}+\frac{\delta}{2},r_{0}+\delta)
such that it holds

(2.36)

\displaystyle\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{-})|^{2}}{r_{-}^{2}}+\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{+})|^{2}}{r_{+}^{2}}\leq\frac{4}{\delta}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Here
\delta\in(0,r_{0})
is a constant to be determined later. Noting

\displaystyle\Im\langle F,\frac{w(\chi_{(0,r_{-})}-\chi_{(r_{+},\infty)})}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Im\langle-[\partial_{r}^{2}w-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})w]+i\beta_{k}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\lambda)w,\frac{w(\chi_{(0,r_{-})}-\chi_{(r_{+},\infty)})}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Im\langle-\partial_{r}^{2}w+i\beta_{k}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\lambda)w,\frac{w(\chi_{(0,r_{-})}-\chi_{(r_{+},\infty)})}{r^{2}}\rangle,

we obtain

\displaystyle|\beta_{k}|(\int_{0}^{r_{-}}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}})|\frac{w(r)}{r}|^{2}dr+\int_{r_{+}}^{\infty}(\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{r^{2}})|\frac{w(r)}{r}|^{2}dr)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{-})w(r_{-})|}{r_{-}^{2}}+\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{+})w(r_{+})|}{r_{+}^{2}}.

With
r_{-}\in(r_{0}-\delta,r_{0}-\frac{\delta}{2})
and
r_{+}\in(r_{0}+\frac{\delta}{2},r_{0}+\delta)
, it holds

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}=\frac{(r_{0}-r)(r_{0}+r)}{r^{2}r_{0}^{2}}\geq\frac{r_{0}-r}{r^{2}r_{0}}\gtrsim\frac{\delta}{r^{2}r_{0}}\gtrsim\frac{\delta}{r_{0}^{3}},\quad\textrm{for any}\ r\in(0,r_{-}).

Similarly, it can be verified that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{r^{2}}=\frac{(r-r_{0})(r+r_{0})}{r^{2}r_{0}^{2}}\geq\frac{r-r_{0}}{r^{2}r_{0}}\gtrsim\frac{\delta}{r^{2}r_{0}}\gtrsim\frac{\delta}{r_{0}^{3}},\quad\textrm{for any}\ r\in(r_{+},2r_{0}),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{r^{2}}=\frac{(r-r_{0})(r+r_{0})}{r^{2}r_{0}^{2}}\approx\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}\gtrsim\frac{\delta}{r_{0}^{3}},\quad\textrm{for any}\ r\geq 2r_{0}.

Combining all inequalities above, we deduce

\displaystyle\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{-})w(r_{-})|}{r_{-}^{2}}+\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{+})w(r_{+})|}{r_{+}^{2}}

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle\frac{|\beta_{k}|\delta}{r_{0}^{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}((0,r_{-})\cup(r_{+},\infty))}^{2}.

Hence
\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}
can be bounded by

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}((0,r_{-})\cup(r_{+},\infty))}^{2}+\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(r_{-},r_{+})}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|\delta}(\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{-})w(r_{-})|}{r_{-}^{2}}+\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{+})w(r_{+})|}{r_{+}^{2}})+\delta\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

Together with (
2.36
), Lemma
A.2
and Lemma
2.3
, this gives

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|\delta}(\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\delta^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}})+\delta\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|\delta}(\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{5}{4}}}{\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}})+\delta\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}.

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\gtrsim\min\{\frac{|\beta_{k}|\delta}{r_{0}^{3}},(\frac{|\beta_{k}|\delta^{\frac{3}{2}}}{r_{0}^{3}})^{\frac{4}{3}},\delta^{-2}\}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Taking
\delta=(\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|})^{\frac{1}{3}}
, and noting
|\beta_{k}|\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\delta=(\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|})^{\frac{1}{3}}\leq r_{0}.

With the basic equality
\frac{|\beta_{k}|\delta}{r_{0}^{3}}=(\frac{|\beta_{k}|\delta^{\frac{3}{2}}}{r_{0}^{3}})^{\frac{4}{3}}=\delta^{-2}
, we arrive at

(2.37)

\displaystyle\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\gtrsim|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Plugging in (
2.31
), (
2.32
), (
2.33
), (
2.35
) and (
2.37
), we therefore establish the following resolvent estimate from
L^{2}(\frac{1}{r^{2}})
to
L^{2}(\frac{1}{r^{2}})

(2.38)

\displaystyle|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}.

In addition, by Lemma
2.3
and (
2.38
) we deduce

\displaystyle\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim|\beta_{k}|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{F}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

This completes the proof of Proposition
2.8
.
∎

Finally, we are ready to derive the resolvent estimates from
L^{2}(\frac{1}{r^{2}})
to
H^{-1}(\frac{1}{r^{2}})
.

Proposition 2.9
.

For any
|k|\geq 1
,
\lambda\in\mathbb{R}
and
w\in D_{k}
, there exist constants
C,c_{2}>0
independent with
k,\beta_{k},\lambda
, such that the following inequality holds

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\frac{F}{r}-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}.

Proof.

By Lemma
2.3
we obtain

\displaystyle\Re\langle F,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle=\Re\langle F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\gtrsim\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\langle(\frac{k^{2}}{r^{2}}+r^{2})w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle\gtrsim\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}^{2},

with
c_{2}>0
to be determined later. It follows directly that

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\lesssim\|\frac{F}{r}-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Thus we have

(2.39)

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim\|\frac{F}{r}-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}.

We proceed in the similar fashion as in Proposition
2.6
to show
|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\frac{F}{r}-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}
. Likewise, we divide the proof into different cases. If
\lambda\leq 0
, using Lemma
2.3
we obtain

\displaystyle\Re\langle F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\gtrsim\langle(\frac{k^{2}}{r^{2}}+r^{2})w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle.

This together with

\displaystyle|\Im\langle F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle|\geq|\beta_{k}|\langle\frac{1}{r^{2}}w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle,

and (
2.39
) gives

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\gtrsim\langle(r^{2}+\frac{|\beta_{k}|}{r^{2}})w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle\geq|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Then we can deduce

\displaystyle C(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\geq|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Notice
|\beta_{k}|\geq 1
. Choose
c_{2}>0
sufficiently small satisfying
Cc_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we conclude

(2.40)

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}\gtrsim|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

If
\lambda>0
and
|\beta_{k}|\leq k^{2}
, it follows from Lemma
2.3
and (
2.39
)

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\geq

\displaystyle\Re\langle F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle\langle(\frac{k^{2}}{r^{2}}+r^{2})w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle\geq|k|\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\geq|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Note
|\beta_{k}|\geq 1
. Choose
c_{2}>0
sufficiently small satisfying
Cc_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we conclude

(2.41)

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}\gtrsim|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

If
\lambda>0
and
|\beta_{k}|\geq k^{2}
, the discussion can be separated into three cases. Recall
r_{0}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}}
.

(1)

Case of
|\beta_{k}|\leq r_{0}^{4}
.
Recall that Lemma
2.3
yields

(2.42)

\displaystyle\Re\langle F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\gtrsim\langle(\frac{k^{2}}{r^{2}}+r^{2})w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle.

Since

\displaystyle\Im\langle F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle=\Im\langle-w^{\prime\prime},\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle+\beta_{k}\langle(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}})w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Im\langle 2w^{\prime},\frac{w}{r^{3}}\rangle+\beta_{k}\langle(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}})w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle,

and note (
2.39
), it holds

\displaystyle|\beta_{k}|\langle(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}})w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle\leq

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+2\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+2\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Combining with (
2.42
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\gtrsim\langle[r^{2}+|\beta_{k}|(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}})]w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle.

Observing

\displaystyle r^{2}+|\beta_{k}|(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}})\geq 2|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{2}}-\frac{|\beta_{k}|}{r_{0}^{2}}\geq|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{2}},

we deduce

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\gtrsim|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Recall
|\beta_{k}|\geq 1
. Choose
c_{2}>0
sufficiently small so that
Cc_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we conclude

(2.43)

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}\gtrsim|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(2)

Case of
r_{0}^{4}\leq|\beta_{k}|\leq r_{0}^{6}
.
Then
r_{0}\geq 1
. Utilizing Lemma
2.3
and (
2.39
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\frac{F}{r}-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\gtrsim

\displaystyle\Re\langle F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle\langle(\frac{k^{2}}{r^{2}}+r^{2})w,\frac{w}{r^{2}}\rangle\geq\|r\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\geq\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{4}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(\frac{r_{0}}{2},\infty)}^{2}.

We move to estimate
\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,\frac{r_{0}}{2})}^{2}
. Pick
r_{-}\in(\frac{r_{0}}{2}-\frac{1}{r_{0}},\frac{r_{0}}{2})
such that the following inequality holds

\displaystyle\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{-})|^{2}}{r_{-}^{2}}\leq r_{0}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We construct a cutoff function

\displaystyle\rho(r)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &1,\quad r\in(0,\frac{r_{0}}{2}-\frac{2}{r_{0}}),\\
&\sin\big(\frac{\pi}{2}\frac{1}{r_{-}-(\frac{r_{0}}{2}-\frac{2}{r_{0}})}(r_{-}-r)\big),\quad r\in(\frac{r_{0}}{2}-\frac{2}{r_{0}},r_{-}),\\
&0,\quad r\geq r_{-}.\end{aligned}\right.

Starting from with the following basic equality

\displaystyle\Im\langle F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w,\frac{w\rho(r)}{r^{2}}\rangle=

\displaystyle\Im\langle-[\partial_{r}^{2}w-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})w]+i\beta_{k}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\lambda)w,\frac{w\rho(r)}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Im\langle-\partial_{r}^{2}w+i\beta_{k}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\lambda)w,\frac{w\rho(r)}{r^{2}}\rangle,

we obtain

\displaystyle|\beta_{k}|\langle(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\lambda)w,\frac{w\rho(r)}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}\|\frac{w\rho(r)}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+r_{0}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Thus with
\lambda=\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}
and
r_{-}\in(\frac{r_{0}}{2}-\frac{1}{2r_{0}},\frac{r_{0}}{2})
we have

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}\|\frac{w\rho(r)}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+r_{0}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle|\beta_{k}|\langle(\frac{4}{r_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}})w,\frac{w\rho(r)}{r^{2}}\rangle\geq\frac{|\beta_{k}|}{r_{0}^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,\frac{r_{0}}{2}-\frac{2}{r_{0}})}^{2}.

Note

\displaystyle\|\frac{\rho w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+r_{0}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}.

Combining with (
2.39
), we deduce

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}\|\frac{w\rho(r)}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+r_{0}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}(\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+r_{0}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})+r_{0}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+r_{0}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle+r_{0}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|\frac{F}{r}-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Therefore, with Lemma
A.2
, we now can bound
\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}
by

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(0,\frac{r_{0}}{2}-\frac{2}{r_{0}})}^{2}+\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(\frac{r_{0}}{2}-\frac{2}{r_{0}},\frac{r_{0}}{2})}^{2}+\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(\frac{r_{0}}{2},\infty)}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{|\beta_{k}|}\Big[\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+r_{0}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle+\sqrt{c_{2}}r_{0}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]+\frac{1}{r_{0}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}(\|\frac{F}{r}-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{|\beta_{k}|}\Big[\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+r_{0}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle+\sqrt{c_{2}}r_{0}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]+\frac{1}{r_{0}}\|(\frac{w}{r})^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}(\|\frac{F}{r}-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big\{\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{|\beta_{k}|}\Big[\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+r_{0}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle+\sqrt{c_{2}}r_{0}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{r_{0}}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}(\|\frac{F}{r}-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\Big\}.

The assumption
r_{0}^{4}\leq|\beta_{k}|\leq r_{0}^{6}
implies

\displaystyle C(c_{2}\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{2}{3}}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{5}{6}}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}\frac{|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}}{r_{0}}+c_{2}\frac{|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}}{r_{0}^{2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{-\frac{1}{6}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{-\frac{1}{12}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}+c_{2}).

Recalling
|\beta_{k}|\geq 1
, we can choose
c_{2}>0
small enough to obtain

\displaystyle C(c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{-\frac{1}{6}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{-\frac{1}{12}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}+c_{2})\leq\frac{1}{2}.

Therefore, we deduce

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}\gtrsim\min\{\frac{|\beta_{k}|}{r_{0}^{2}},(\frac{|\beta_{k}|}{r_{0}^{3}})^{2},r_{0}^{2}\}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

By
r_{0}^{4}\leq|\beta_{k}|\leq r_{0}^{6}
, i.e.
\frac{|\beta_{k}|^{2}}{r_{0}^{6}}\geq\frac{|\beta_{k}|}{r_{0}^{2}}\geq r_{0}^{2}
, we arrive at

(2.44)

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}\gtrsim r_{0}^{2}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\geq|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(3)

Case of
|\beta_{k}|\geq r_{0}^{6}
.
We pick up
r_{-}\in(r_{0}-\delta,r_{0}-\frac{\delta}{2})
and
r_{+}\in(r_{0}+\frac{\delta}{2},r_{0}+\delta)
such that the following inequality holds

\displaystyle\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{-})|^{2}}{r_{-}^{2}}+\frac{|w^{\prime}(r_{+})|^{2}}{r_{+}^{2}}\leq\frac{4}{\delta}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

Here
\delta\in(0,r_{0})
is a constant to be determined later. As in Proposition
2.6
, the
H^{-1}
estimate requires the cutoff function
\rho(r)
is of
C^{1}
regularity. Let us introduce

\displaystyle\rho(r)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &1,\quad r\in(0,r_{0}-2\delta),\\
&\sin\big(\frac{\pi}{2}\frac{1}{r_{-}-(r_{0}-2\delta)}(r_{-}-r)\big),\quad r\in(r_{0}-2\delta,r_{-}),\\
&0,\quad r\in(r_{-},r_{+}),\\
&\sin\big(\frac{\pi}{2}\frac{1}{r_{0}+2\delta-r_{+}}(r_{+}-r)\big),\quad r\in(r_{+},r_{0}+2\delta),\\
&-1,\quad r\geq r_{0}+2\delta.\end{aligned}\right.

Write

\displaystyle\Im\langle F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w,\frac{w\rho(r)}{r^{2}}\rangle=

\displaystyle\Im\langle-[\partial_{r}^{2}w-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})w]+i\beta_{k}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\lambda)w,\frac{w\rho(r)}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Im\langle-\partial_{r}^{2}w+i\beta_{k}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\lambda)w,\frac{w\rho(r)}{r^{2}}\rangle.

This gives

\displaystyle|\beta_{k}|(\int_{0}^{r_{0}-2\delta}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}})\frac{|w(r)|^{2}}{r^{2}}dr+\int_{r_{0}+2\delta}^{\infty}(\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{r^{2}})|w(r)|^{2}dr)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}\|\frac{\rho w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+\delta^{-1}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}.

With
r_{-}\in(r_{0}-\delta,r_{0}-\frac{\delta}{2})
and
r_{+}\in(r_{0}+\frac{\delta}{2},r_{0}+\delta)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}=\frac{(r_{0}-r)(r_{0}+r)}{r^{2}r_{0}^{2}}\geq\frac{r_{0}-r}{r^{2}r_{0}}\gtrsim\frac{\delta}{r^{2}r_{0}}\gtrsim\frac{\delta}{r_{0}^{3}},\quad\textrm{for any}\ r\in(0,r_{-}),

Analogously, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{r^{2}}=\frac{(r-r_{0})(r+r_{0})}{r^{2}r_{0}^{2}}\geq\frac{r-r_{0}}{r^{2}r_{0}}\gtrsim\frac{\delta}{r^{2}r_{0}}\gtrsim\frac{\delta}{r_{0}^{3}},\quad\textrm{for any}\ r\in(r_{+},2r_{0}),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{r^{2}}=\frac{(r-r_{0})(r+r_{0})}{r^{2}r_{0}^{2}}\approx\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}\gtrsim\frac{\delta}{r_{0}^{3}},\quad\textrm{for any}\ r\geq 2r_{0}.

Combining inequalities above, we deduce

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}\|\frac{\rho w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+\delta^{-1}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle|\beta_{k}|(\int_{0}^{r_{0}-2\delta}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}})|\frac{w}{r}|^{2}dr+\int_{r_{0}+2\delta}^{\infty}(\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{r^{2}})|\frac{w}{r}|^{2}dr)

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle\frac{|\beta_{k}|\delta}{r_{0}^{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}\big((0,r_{0}-2\delta)\cup(r_{0}+2\delta,\infty)\big)}^{2}.

Noting

\displaystyle\|\frac{\rho w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+\delta^{-1}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}},

together with (
2.39
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}\|\frac{\rho w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+\delta^{-1}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}(\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+\delta^{-1}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})+\delta^{-1}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\delta^{-1}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle+\delta^{-1}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|\frac{F}{r}-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Therefore, with Lemma
A.2
, we can estimate
\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}
as below

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}((0,r_{0}-2\delta)\cup(r_{0}+2\delta,\infty))}^{2}+\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}(r_{0}-2\delta,r_{0}+2\delta)}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|\delta}\Big[\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\delta^{-1}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle+\delta^{-1}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|\frac{F}{r}-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]+\delta\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|\delta}\Big[\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+\delta^{-1}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle+\delta^{-1}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|\frac{F}{r}-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]+\delta\|(\frac{w}{r})^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|\delta}\Big[\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\delta^{-1}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})+\sqrt{c_{2}}\delta^{-1}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]+\delta(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big\{\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|\delta}\Big[\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\delta^{-1}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})+\sqrt{c_{2}}\delta^{-1}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]+\delta(\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}})\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\Big\}.

Take
\delta=(\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|})^{\frac{1}{3}}
, then the condition
|\beta_{k}|\geq\max\{r_{0}^{6},1\}
yields

\displaystyle\delta=(\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|})^{\frac{1}{3}}\leq r_{0}.

Recall
|\beta_{k}|\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle C(c_{2}\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{2}{3}}\delta}+\sqrt{c_{2}}\frac{r_{0}^{3}}{|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{5}{6}}\delta^{2}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}\delta|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}})=C(c_{2}\frac{r_{0}^{2}}{|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}}+2\sqrt{c_{2}}\frac{r_{0}}{|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}})\leq C(c_{2}+2\sqrt{c_{2}}).

If
c_{2}>0
satisfies

\displaystyle C(c_{2}+2\sqrt{c_{2}})\leq\frac{1}{2},

we can conclude

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}\gtrsim\min\{\frac{|\beta_{k}|\delta}{r_{0}^{3}},(\frac{|\beta_{k}|\delta^{2}}{r_{0}^{3}})^{2},\delta^{-2}\}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

With the basic equality
\frac{|\beta_{k}|\delta}{r_{0}^{3}}=(\frac{|\beta_{k}|\delta^{2}}{r_{0}^{3}})^{2}=\delta^{-2}
, we arrive at

(2.45)

\displaystyle\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}\gtrsim|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Combining (
2.40
), (
2.41
), (
2.43
), (
2.44
) and (
2.45
), we therefore establish the following resolvent estimate from
L^{2}(\frac{1}{r^{2}})
to
H^{-1}(\frac{1}{r^{2}})

\displaystyle|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}.

Applying (
2.39
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim\|\frac{F}{r}-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}+\sqrt{c_{2}}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}.

This completes the proof of Proposition
2.9
.
∎

The following proposition follows directly from Proposition
2.8
and Proposition
2.9
if we choose
c_{2}>0
small enough.

Proposition 2.10
.

For any
|k|\geq 1
,
\lambda\in\mathbb{R}
and
w\in D_{k}
, there exist constants
C,c_{2}>0
which independent with
k,\beta_{k},\lambda
, such that

\displaystyle|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{L^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{H^{1}}+|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\frac{F-c_{2}|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}w}{r}\|_{H^{-1}}.

2.4.
Sharpness of the resolvent bound
|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}

In the last subsection, we prove that the resolvent bound
|\beta_{k}|^{\frac{1}{3}}
in Proposition
2.5
is sharp. We have

Lemma 2.11
.

There exist
\lambda\in\mathbb{R}
and non-zero
w\in C_{0}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}_{+},dr)
such that it holds

\displaystyle\|F\|_{L^{2}}\leq C|\beta_{1}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|w\|_{L^{2}}.

Proof.

Choose
\lambda=\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}
and
\beta_{1}=r_{0}^{6}
for some
r_{0}\geq 1
. We pick up a function
\eta(r)
as below

\displaystyle\eta(r)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &r(1-r),\quad 0\leq r\leq 1,\\
&0,\quad r\geq 1.\end{aligned}\right.

We construct

\displaystyle w(r)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &(r-r_{0})(r_{0}+\frac{1}{r_{0}}-r),\quad r_{0}\leq r\leq r_{0}+\frac{1}{r_{0}},\\
&0,\quad r\in(0,r_{0})\cup(r_{0}+\frac{1}{r_{0}},\infty).\end{aligned}\right.

One can verify

\displaystyle\|w\|_{L^{2}}=r_{0}^{-\frac{5}{2}}\|\eta\|_{L^{2}},\quad w^{\prime\prime}=-2.

Then it holds

\displaystyle\|-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{3}{4}\frac{1}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]w\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim(1+r_{0}^{2})\|w\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim r_{0}^{2}\|w\|_{L^{2}}.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle|\beta_{1}|\|(\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}}-\frac{1}{r^{2}})w\|_{L^{2}}=|\beta_{1}|\|\frac{(r-r_{0})(r+r_{0})}{r_{0}^{2}r^{2}}w\|_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{|\beta_{1}|}{r_{0}}\|\frac{r+r_{0}}{r_{0}^{2}r^{2}}w\|_{L^{2}}\approx\frac{|\beta_{1}|}{r_{0}^{4}}\|w\|_{L^{2}}.

Thus we deduce

\displaystyle\|-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{3}{4}\frac{1}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]w+i\beta_{1}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim(r_{0}^{2}+\frac{|\beta_{1}|}{r_{0}^{4}})\|w\|_{L^{2}}.

Together with
|\beta_{1}|=r_{0}^{6}
this implies

\displaystyle\|-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{3}{4}\frac{1}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]w+i\beta_{1}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\frac{1}{r_{0}^{2}})w\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim r_{0}^{2}\|w\|_{L^{2}}=|\beta_{1}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|w\|_{L^{2}}.

∎

3.
Space-time estimate for the linearized Navier-Stokes equations

In this section, for the linearized 2D Navier-Stokes equation in the vorticity formulation (
1.34
), we establish the space-time estimate. We consider

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{\tau}w_{k}+L_{k}w_{k}+f_{1}-\partial_{r}f_{2}=0,\\
&w_{k}(0)=w_{k}|_{\tau=0},\quad w_{k}|_{r=0,\infty}=0.\end{aligned}\right.

Here

L_{k}=-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]+i\frac{kB}{r^{2}}
and
f_{1},f_{2}
are nonlinear terms in Section
1.4
.

We also introduce the space-time norm

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L^{p}L^{2}}=\big\|\|g\|_{L_{r}^{2}}\big\|_{L_{t}^{p}(0,\infty)},\quad\|g\|_{L^{p}X}=\big\|\|g\|_{X}\big\|_{L_{t}^{p}(0,\infty)},

where

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L_{r}^{2}}=(\int_{0}^{\infty}|g(r)|^{2}dr)^{\frac{1}{2}},\quad\|g\|_{X}=(\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{|g(r)|^{2}}{r^{2}}dr)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

3.1.
Pseudospectral bound and semigroup bound

3.1.1.
Pseudospectral bound.

Recall in
[
34
]
an operator
L
in a Hilbert space
H
is called accretive if

\displaystyle\Re\langle Lf,f\rangle\geq 0,\quad\textrm{for any}\ f\in D(L).

The operator
L
is called m-accretive if in addition all
\lambda>0
belong to the resolvent set of
L
(see
[
27
]
for more details). The
pseudospectral bound
of
L
is defined to be

(3.1)

\displaystyle\Psi(L)=\inf\{\|(L-i\lambda)f\|:f\in D(L),\lambda\in\mathbb{R},\|f\|=1\}.

Recall the operator
L_{k}
in (
1.34
) is given by

\displaystyle L_{k}w=

\displaystyle-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]w+i\frac{kB}{r^{2}}w.

And we will use the weighted-
L^{2}
space
X
. Its norm is expressed as
\|w\|_{X}=(\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{|w|^{2}}{r^{2}}dr)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Note that
-\partial_{r}^{2}
is a operator with the compact resolvent. Since
L_{k}
is a relatively compact perturbation for
-\partial_{r}^{2}
in the domain
D_{k}
, it is hence clear that the operators
L_{k}
has the compact resolvent and only point spectrum.

Employing Lemma
2.1
and Lemma
2.3
we obtain

\displaystyle\Re\langle(L_{k}-i\lambda)w,w\rangle_{L^{2}}\gtrsim|k|\|w\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\quad\Re\langle(L_{k}-i\lambda)w,w\rangle_{X}\gtrsim|k|\|w\|_{X}^{2},\quad\textrm{for any}\ \lambda\in\mathbb{R}.

This indicates that
L_{k}
is accretive, and is also m-accretive. Recall in Proposition
2.5
and Proposition
2.8
we establish the resolvent estimates

\displaystyle\|(L_{k}-i\lambda)w\|_{L^{2}}\geq C|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|w\|_{L^{2}},\quad\|(L_{k}-i\lambda)w\|_{X}\geq C|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|w\|_{X}.

This implies the following lemma for the pseudospectral bounds of
L_{k}
:

Lemma 3.1
.

Let
\Psi
be defined as in (
3.1
), it holds
\Psi(L_{k}(L^{2}\rightarrow L^{2}))\geq C|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}
,
\Psi(L_{k}(X\rightarrow X))\geq C|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}
.

3.1.2.
Semigroup bounds.

To prove decay estimates, we appeal to semigroup bounds. To obtain them from pseudospectral bounds, we employ the following Gearhart-Pr
\ddot{u}
ss type lemma established by Wei in
[
39
]
.

Lemma 3.2

(Wei
[
39
]
)
.

Let
L
be a m-accretive operator in a Hilbert space
H
. Then we have

\displaystyle\|e^{-tL}\|_{H}\leq e^{-t\Psi+\frac{\pi}{2}},\quad\textrm{for any}\ t\geq 0.

We are now ready to study the homogeneous linear equation

(3.2)

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}w_{k}^{l}+L_{k}w_{k}^{l}=0,\quad w_{k}^{l}(0)=w_{k}(0).

Utilizing the language of semigroup theory, we can write

\displaystyle w_{k}^{l}(\tau)=e^{-\tau L_{k}}w_{k}(0).

And we obtain

Proposition 3.3
.

Let
w_{k}^{l}
be a solution of (
3.2
) with
w_{k}(0)\in L^{2}
and
w_{k}(0)\in X
. Then for any
|k|\geq 1(k\in\mathbb{Z})
, there exist constants
C,c_{3}>0
being independent of
B,k
, such that the following inequalities hold

(3.3)

\displaystyle\|w_{k}^{l}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}}\leq Ce^{-c_{3}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}},\quad\textrm{for any}\ \tau\geq 0,

(3.4)

\displaystyle\|w_{k}^{l}(\tau)\|_{X}\leq Ce^{-c_{3}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X},\quad\textrm{for any}\ \tau\geq 0.

Moreover, for any
|k|\geq 1(k\in\mathbb{Z})
and any
c^{\prime}\in(0,c_{3})
, it holds

(3.5)

\displaystyle|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{-c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(3.6)

\displaystyle|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{-c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}\leq C\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X}^{2}.

Proof.

To derive the semigroup bounds (
3.3
) and (
3.4
), we can directly apply Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.2
. We proceed to use (
3.3
). For any
c^{\prime}\in(0,c_{3})
we deduce

\displaystyle 2c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq 2Cc^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}e^{-2(c_{3}-c^{\prime})|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Integrating with respect to
\tau
on both sides yields

\displaystyle 2c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}2Cc^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}e^{-2(c_{2}-c^{\prime})|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}d\tau\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

In a similar fashion, by (
3.3
), for any
c^{\prime}\in(0,c_{3})
we have

\displaystyle 2c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}(\tau)\|_{X}^{2}\leq 2Cc^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}e^{-2(c_{2}-c^{\prime})|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X}^{2},

which implies

\displaystyle 2c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}2Cc^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}e^{-2(c_{2}-c^{\prime})|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}d\tau\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X}^{2}\lesssim\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X}^{2}.

This completes the proof of Proposition
3.3
.
∎

3.2.
Space-time estimates for non-zero frequency

Recall the full nonlinear equation (
1.34
) as follows

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{\tau}w_{k}+L_{k}w_{k}+f_{1}-\partial_{r}f_{2}=0,\\
&w_{k}(0)=w_{k}|_{\tau=0},\quad w_{k}|_{r=0,\infty}=0,\end{aligned}\right.

where
L_{k}=-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]+i\frac{kB}{r^{2}}
and

\displaystyle f_{1}=ik\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}w_{l}\frac{\partial_{r}\breve{\varphi}_{k-l}}{r}+\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}i(k-l)(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{2r^{2}})w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{k-l},\quad f_{2}=\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{k-l}}{r},

with
(\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}-\frac{k^{2}}{r^{2}})\breve{\varphi}_{k}=fw_{k}
,
f=\frac{e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}
.

We decompose the solution
w_{k}
into two parts: let
w_{k}=w_{k}^{l}+w_{k}^{n}
so that
w_{k}^{l}
satisfies the below homogeneous linear equation with initial data
w_{k}(0)

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}w_{k}^{l}+L_{k}w_{k}^{l}=0,\quad w_{k}^{l}(0)=w_{k}(0),

and
w_{k}^{n}
satisfies the following inhomogeneous linear equation with zero initial data

(3.7)

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}w_{k}^{n}+L_{k}w_{k}^{n}+f_{1}-\partial_{r}f_{2}=0,\quad w_{k}^{n}(0)=0.

We first establish the following space-time estimate for the linear part
w_{k}^{l}
in
L^{2}
space.

Lemma 3.4
.

Let
w_{k}^{l}
be a solution to (
3.2
) with initial data
w_{k}(0)\in L^{2}
,
c_{3}
be the same as in Proposition
3.3
and
c^{\prime}\in(0,c_{3})
. For any
|k|\geq 1(k\in\mathbb{Z})
, it holds

(3.8)

\displaystyle\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Proof.

Employing integration by parts we obtain

\displaystyle\Re\langle\partial_{\tau}w_{k}^{l}-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]w_{k}^{l}+\frac{ikB}{r^{2}}w_{k}^{l},w_{k}^{l}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\langle-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]w_{k}^{l},w_{k}^{l}\rangle=0.

Together with Lemma
2.1
, this gives that there exists a constant
c_{0}>0
such that

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}\|w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+c_{0}(\|\partial_{r}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\leq 0.

Multiplying
e^{2c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}
on both sides, we deduce

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+c_{0}(\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Thus we obtain the space-time estimate of
w_{k}^{l}
as follows

\displaystyle\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+c_{0}\Big(\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Combining with Proposition
3.3
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

This completes the proof of Lemma
3.4
.
∎

Analogously, we derive the space-time estimate for the linear part
w_{k}^{l}
in weighted
L^{2}
space.

Lemma 3.5
.

Let
w_{k}^{l}
be a solution to (
3.2
) with initial data
w_{k}(0)\in X
,
c_{3}
be the same as in Proposition
3.3
and
c^{\prime}\in(0,c_{3})
. For any
|k|\geq 1(k\in\mathbb{Z})
, it holds

(3.9)

\displaystyle\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}X}^{2}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}+\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}\lesssim\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X}^{2}.

Proof.

Via integration by parts we have

\displaystyle\Re\langle\partial_{\tau}w_{k}^{l}-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]w_{k}^{l}+\frac{ikB}{r^{2}}w_{k}^{l},\frac{w_{k}^{l}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|\frac{w_{k}^{l}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\langle-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]w_{k}^{l},\frac{w_{k}^{l}}{r^{2}}\rangle=0.

Together with Lemma
2.3
, this yields that there exists a constant
c_{0}>0
such that

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}\|w_{k}^{l}\|_{X}^{2}+c_{0}\Big(\|\partial_{r}w_{k}^{l}\|_{X}^{2}+\|(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}^{l}\|_{X}^{2}\Big)\leq 0.

Multiplying
e^{2c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}
on both sides, the inequality above becomes

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{X}^{2}+c_{0}(\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}^{l}\|_{X}^{2}+\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}^{l}\|_{X}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{X}^{2}.

This implies the space-time estimate of
w_{k}^{l}
as below

\displaystyle\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}X}^{2}+c_{0}(\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}+\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}+\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X}^{2}.

Plugging in Proposition
3.3
, we conclude

\displaystyle\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}X}^{2}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}+\|e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}\lesssim\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X}^{2}.

This completes the proof of Lemma
3.5
.
∎

Next we establish the space-time estimates for
w_{k}^{n}
, which satisfies the following inhomogeneous equation (
3.7
) with zero initial data

(3.10)

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}w_{k}^{n}+L_{k}w_{k}^{n}+f_{1}-\partial_{r}f_{2}=0,\quad w_{k}^{n}(0)=0.

Multiply both sides by
e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}
, with
c_{2}
being the same constant as in Proposition
2.7
and in Proposition
2.10
. The above inhomogeneous equation then becomes

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}(e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{n})+\Big(L_{k}-c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\Big)(e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{n})+e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(f_{1}-\partial_{r}f_{2})=0,\quad w_{k}^{n}(0)=0.

Define
\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}=e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{n}
and
\tilde{f}_{j}=e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{j}
for
j=1,2
. We then derive the following space-time estimate for the nonlinear part
w_{k}^{n}
in
L^{2}
space.

Lemma 3.6
.

Let
w_{k}^{n}
be a solution to (
3.7
) with zero initial data. For any
|k|\geq 1(k\in\mathbb{Z})
, it holds

\displaystyle\|e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim|kB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

Proof.

We first take the Fourier transform in
\tau
and define
1
1

1

Note that the zero initial condition in (
3.10
) ensures that the Fourier transform is well-defined for
\lambda\in(0,\infty)
.

\displaystyle\hat{w}_{k}^{n}(\lambda,r)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}(\tau,r)e^{-i\tau\lambda}d\tau,\quad F_{j}(\lambda,r,k)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\tilde{f}_{j}(\tau,r,k)e^{-i\tau\lambda}d\tau\quad\textrm{for}\ j=1,2.

The inhomogeneous equation (
3.7
) is transferred into the following resolvent equation

\displaystyle(i\lambda+L_{k}-c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}})\hat{w}_{k}^{n}(\lambda,r)=-F_{1}+\partial_{r}F_{2}.

We further decompose
\hat{w}_{k}^{n}
as
\hat{w}_{k}^{n}=\hat{w}_{k}^{n,1}+\hat{w}_{k}^{n,2}
, where
\hat{w}_{k}^{n,1}
and
\hat{w}_{k}^{n,2}
satisfy

\displaystyle\Big(i\lambda+L_{k}-c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\Big)\hat{w}_{k}^{n,1}=-F_{1}\quad\textrm{and}\quad\Big(i\lambda+L_{k}-c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\Big)\hat{w}_{k}^{n,2}=\partial_{r}F_{2}.

respectively.

Applying Proposition
2.7
, we obtain

\displaystyle|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\partial_{r}\hat{w}_{k}^{n,1}\|_{L^{2}}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\hat{w}_{k}^{n,1}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|F_{1}\|_{L^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\|\partial_{r}\hat{w}_{k}^{n,2}\|_{L^{2}}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\hat{w}_{k}^{n,2}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{r}F_{2}\|_{H^{-1}}\leq C\|F_{2}\|_{L^{2}}.

Combining the above inequalities, we deduce

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\partial_{r}\hat{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\hat{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim|kB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|F_{1}\|_{L^{2}}+|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|F_{2}\|_{L^{2}}.

Therefore, using Plancherel’s theorem, it holds

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\big\|\|\partial_{r}\hat{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}+\big\|\|\hat{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\big\|\|F_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}+|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\big\|\|F_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}

(3.11)

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\tilde{f}_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\tilde{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}.

Via integration by parts, we also have

\displaystyle\Re\langle\partial_{\tau}w_{k}^{n}-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]w_{k}^{n}+\frac{i\beta_{k}}{r^{2}}w_{k}^{n}+f_{1}-\partial_{r}f_{2},e^{2c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{n}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\langle-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}-c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}+\tilde{f}_{1}-\partial_{r}\tilde{f}_{2},\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\rangle=0.

Together with Lemma
2.1
, this yields that there exists a constant
c_{0}>0
such that

\displaystyle c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+c_{0}(\|\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+\Re\langle(\tilde{f}_{1}-\partial_{r}\tilde{f}_{2}),\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+c_{0}(\|\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+\Re\langle\tilde{f}_{1},\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\rangle+\Re\langle\tilde{f}_{2},\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\rangle.

By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2c_{0}\Big(\|\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\|\tilde{f}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\tilde{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\Big)\Big(\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}+|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}\Big)+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Combining with (
3.11
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|\tilde{f}_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\tilde{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}})(\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}})+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}(\|\tilde{f}_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\tilde{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}})^{2}\lesssim|kB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\tilde{f}_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\tilde{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

This completes the proof of Lemma
3.6
.
∎

We then proceed in the same fashion and derive the space-time estimate for the nonlinear part
w_{k}^{n}
in weighted
L^{2}
space.

Lemma 3.7
.

Let
w_{k}^{n}
be a solution to (
3.7
) with zero initial data. For any
|k|\geq 1(k\in\mathbb{Z})
, it holds

\displaystyle\|e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}X}^{2}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}+\|e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\Big(\|e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}+\|e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{f_{2}}{r}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}\Big)+\|e^{c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2},

where
w_{k}^{n}
satisfies the equation (
3.7
).

Proof.

We still take the Fourier transform with respective to
t
and get

\displaystyle\hat{w}_{k}^{n}(\lambda,r)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}(\tau,r)e^{-i\tau\lambda}d\tau,\quad F_{j}(\lambda,r,k)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\tilde{f}_{j}(\tau,r,k)e^{-i\tau\lambda}d\tau\quad\textrm{with}\ j=1,2.

The inhomogeneous equation (
3.7
) then becomes

\displaystyle(i\lambda+L_{k}-c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}})\hat{w}_{k}^{n}(\lambda,r)=-F_{1}+\partial_{r}F_{2}=-F_{1}+r\partial_{r}(\frac{F_{2}}{r})+\frac{F_{2}}{r}.

As in Lemma
3.6
, we decompose
\hat{w}_{k}^{n}=\hat{w}_{k}^{n,1}+\hat{w}_{k}^{n,2}
, where
\hat{w}_{k}^{n,1}
and
\hat{w}_{k}^{n,2}
satisfy

\displaystyle(i\lambda+L_{k}-c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}})\hat{w}_{k}^{n,1}=-F_{1}+\frac{F_{2}}{r}\quad\textrm{and}\quad(i\lambda+L_{k}-c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}})\hat{w}_{k}^{n,2}=r\partial_{r}(\frac{F_{2}}{r}),

respectively.
It follows from Proposition
2.10
that

\displaystyle|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\partial_{r}\hat{w}_{k}^{n,1}\|_{X}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\hat{w}_{k}^{n,1}\|_{X}\leq C\|F_{1}-\frac{F_{2}}{r}\|_{X},

and

\displaystyle\|\partial_{r}\hat{w}_{k}^{n,2}\|_{X}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\hat{w}_{k}^{n,2}\|_{X}\leq C\|\partial_{r}(\frac{F_{2}}{r})\|_{H^{-1}}\leq C\|\frac{F_{2}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|F_{2}\|_{X}.

Hence we obtain

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\partial_{r}\hat{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}+\|\hat{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}\lesssim|kB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|F_{1}-\frac{F_{2}}{r}\|_{X}+|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|F_{2}\|_{X}.

Therefore, utilizing Plancherel’s theorem we deduce

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}X}+\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}X}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\big\|\|\partial_{r}\hat{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}+\big\|\|\hat{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\big\|\|F_{1}-\frac{F_{2}}{r}\|_{X}\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}+|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\big\|\|F_{2}\|_{X}\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}

(3.12)

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\tilde{f}_{1}-\frac{\tilde{f}_{2}}{r}\|_{L^{2}X}+|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\tilde{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}X}.

In addition, integration by parts yields

\displaystyle\Re\langle\partial_{\tau}w_{k}^{n}-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]w_{k}^{n}+\frac{i\beta_{k}}{r^{2}}w_{k}^{n}+f_{1}-\partial_{r}f_{2},e^{2c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}^{n}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\langle-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}-c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}+\tilde{f}_{1}-\partial_{r}\tilde{f}_{2},\frac{\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}}{r^{2}}\rangle=0.

Together with Lemma
2.3
, this implies that there exists a constant
c_{0}>0
such that

\displaystyle c_{2}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}^{2}+c_{0}(\|\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}^{2}+\|(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}^{2})+\Re\langle(\tilde{f}_{1}-\partial_{r}\tilde{f}_{2}),\frac{\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}^{2}+c_{0}(\|\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}^{2}+\|(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}^{2})+\Re\langle\frac{\tilde{f}_{1}}{r},\frac{\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}}{r}\rangle-\Re\langle\frac{\tilde{f}_{2}}{r},\frac{\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}}{r}-2\frac{\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}}{r^{2}}\rangle.

Thus we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}^{2}+2c_{0}(\|\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}^{2}+\|(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\tilde{f}_{1}\|_{X}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}+\|\tilde{f}_{2}\|_{X}(\|\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}+\|\frac{\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}}{r}\|_{X})+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{X}^{2}.

With (
3.12
), we conclude

\displaystyle\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}X}^{2}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}+\|\partial_{r}\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}+\|(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)\tilde{w}_{k}^{n}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}(\|\tilde{f}_{1}\|_{L^{2}X}+\|\frac{\tilde{f}_{2}}{r}\|_{L^{2}X})^{2}+\|\tilde{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}X}^{2}.

This completes the proof of Lemma
3.7
.
∎

Now we can state the two key conclusions of this section. We call them the space-time estimates for equation (
1.34
).

Proposition 3.8
.

Assume
w_{k}
is a solution to (
1.34
) with
w_{k}(0)\in L^{2}
and
e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}
,
e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2}\in L^{2}L^{2}
for some
c^{\prime}>0
, then there exist constants
C,c>0
independent of
B,k
so that

(3.13)

\displaystyle\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big[|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}\Big].

Proof.

This result follows directly from our prepared Lemma
3.4
and Lemma
3.6
by taking
c=\min\{c^{\prime},c_{2}\}
.
∎

Proposition 3.9
.

Assume
w_{k}
is a solution to (
1.34
) with
w_{k}(0)\in X
and
e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1},e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2},

e^{c^{\prime}|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{f_{2}}{r}\in L^{2}X
for some
c^{\prime}>0
, then there exist constants
C,c>0
independent of
B,k
so that

(3.14)

\displaystyle\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}X}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}\|_{L^{2}X}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}\|_{L^{2}X}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}\|_{L^{2}X}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big[|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}X}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{f_{2}}{r}\|_{L^{2}X})+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}X}+\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X}\Big].

Proof.

This result follows directly from Lemma
3.5
and Lemma
3.7
by taking
c=\min\{c^{\prime},c_{2}\}
.
∎

3.3.
Space-time estimates for zero frequency

Recall from (
1.34
) that
w_{0}
satisfies

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}w_{0}-[\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{1}{4r^{2}}-\frac{r^{2}}{16}+\frac{1}{2})]w_{0}-\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{2r^{2}})w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}+\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il\partial_{r}(\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r})=0,

where
\breve{\varphi}_{k}
verifies
(\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}-\frac{k^{2}}{r^{2}})\breve{\varphi}_{k}=fw_{k}
with
f=\frac{e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}
. For simplicity, we denote the nonlinear terms as

\displaystyle N=-\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{2r^{2}})w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}+\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il\partial_{r}(\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r}).

And we obtain

Proposition 3.10
.

Let
w_{0}
be the solution to (
1.34
) with initial data
\frac{w_{0}(0)}{r}\in L^{2}
, then
w_{0}(\tau)
obeys

\displaystyle\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|\frac{\partial_{r}w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|w_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|l|\|w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|l|\|\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\frac{w_{0}(0)}{r}\|_{L^{2}}.

Proof.

For any
C^{2}
function
g
, one can check

\displaystyle-w_{0}^{\prime\prime}=-g^{-1}\partial_{r}[r^{2}g^{2}\partial_{r}(r^{-2}g^{-1}w_{0})]-\frac{2}{r}w_{0}^{\prime}-g^{-1}[g^{\prime\prime}+(\frac{2}{r}g)^{\prime}]w_{0}.

We will need a real-valued function
g
satisfying

(3.15)

\displaystyle\frac{g^{\prime\prime}}{g}+\frac{2}{r}\frac{g^{\prime}}{g}=-\frac{3}{r^{2}}.

The existence of
g
is guaranteed by Lemma
A.1
in the Appendix (with
A=2,B=-3
). Employing (
2.9
) and (
3.15
), we deduce

\displaystyle 0=

\displaystyle\langle\partial_{\tau}w_{0}-[\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{1}{4r^{2}}-\frac{r^{2}}{16}+\frac{1}{2})]w_{0}+N,\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|rg\partial_{r}(r^{-2}g^{-1}w_{0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-3\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\langle g^{-1}[g^{\prime\prime}+(\frac{2}{r}g)^{\prime}]w_{0},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle(-\frac{1}{4r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})w_{0},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle+\Re\langle N,\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|rg\partial_{r}(r^{-2}g^{-1}w_{0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\langle g^{-1}(g^{\prime\prime}+\frac{2}{r}g^{\prime})w_{0},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle(-\frac{5}{4r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})w_{0},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle+\Re\langle N,\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|rg\partial_{r}(r^{-2}g^{-1}w_{0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\langle(\frac{7}{4r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})w_{0},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\Re\langle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{2r^{2}})w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle-\Re\langle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r},\frac{\partial_{r}w_{0}}{r^{2}}-\frac{2}{r^{3}}w_{0}\rangle.

Note that there exists
c_{0}>0
such that
\frac{7}{4r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2}\geq c_{0}(\frac{1}{r^{2}}+r^{2})
, we hence obtain

(3.16)

\displaystyle 0\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|rg\partial_{r}(r^{-2}g^{-1}w_{0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+c_{0}\langle(\frac{1}{r^{2}}+r^{2})w_{0},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\Re\langle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{2r^{2}})w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle-\Re\langle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r},\frac{\partial_{r}w_{0}}{r^{2}}-\frac{2}{r^{3}}w_{0}\rangle.

In addition, we also find

(3.17)

\displaystyle 0=

\displaystyle\langle\partial_{\tau}w_{0}-[\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{1}{4r^{2}}-\frac{r^{2}}{16}+\frac{1}{2})]w_{0},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\Re\langle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{2r^{2}})w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle-\Re\langle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r},\frac{\partial_{r}w_{0}}{r^{2}}-\frac{2}{r^{3}}w_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\frac{\partial_{r}w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\frac{13}{4}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{16}\|w_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\Re\langle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{2r^{2}})w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle-\Re\langle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r},\frac{\partial_{r}w_{0}}{r^{2}}-\frac{2}{r^{3}}w_{0}\rangle.

For any
C_{0}>0
, adding
C_{0}\times\eqref{zero mode-1}
and (
3.17
), one obtains

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}C_{0}\partial_{\tau}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{0}\|rg\partial_{r}(r^{-2}g^{-1}w_{0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{0}c_{0}\langle(\frac{1}{r^{2}}+r^{2})w_{0},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\frac{\partial_{r}w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\frac{13}{4}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{16}\|w_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(C_{0}+1)\Big(\Re\langle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{2r^{2}})w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle+\Re\langle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r},\frac{\partial_{r}w_{0}}{r^{2}}-\frac{2}{r^{3}}w_{0}\rangle\Big).

Choose
C_{0}
being large enough such that

\displaystyle\frac{C_{0}c_{0}}{2}\langle(\frac{1}{r^{2}}+r^{2})w_{0},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle\geq\frac{13}{4}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Then we deduce

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\frac{\partial_{r}w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\langle(\frac{1}{r^{2}}+r^{2})w_{0},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\langle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{2r^{2}})w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle|+|\langle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r},\frac{\partial_{r}w_{0}}{r^{2}}-\frac{2}{r^{3}}w_{0}\rangle|.

This yields

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\frac{\partial_{r}w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\langle(\frac{1}{r^{2}}+r^{2})w_{0},\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{2r^{2}})w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Hence we obtain

\displaystyle\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\frac{\partial_{r}w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|w_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|l|\|w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|l|\|\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\frac{w_{0}(0)}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

And this completes the proof of Proposition
3.10
.
∎

4.
Nonlinear stability

This section is devoted to the proof of the main Theorem
4.1
. For the 2D Navier-Stokes equation, it is well-known that smooth initial data could lead to smooth global solutions. Here we are interested in solutions’ asymptotically behaviors, especially for initial data close to the Taylor-Couette flow. We first recall the energy functional
E_{k}
(
1.28
):

\displaystyle E_{k}=

\displaystyle\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+|k|\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+|k|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}\Big)+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}},\quad|k|\geq 1,

\displaystyle E_{0}=

\displaystyle|B|^{\frac{1}{6}}(\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|w_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}),\quad k=0,

where
w_{k}
is a solution of the nonlinear vorticity formulation (
1.34
).

Before we provide the proof, let us recall the nonlinear vorticity formulation (
1.34
) from Section
1.4
:

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{\tau}w_{k}+L_{k}w_{k}+f_{1}-\partial_{r}f_{2}=0,\\
&w_{k}(0)=w_{k}|_{\tau=0},\quad w_{k}|_{r=0,\infty}=0,\end{aligned}\right.

where
L_{k}=-[\partial_{r}^{2}-(\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}+\frac{r^{2}}{16}-\frac{1}{2})]+i\frac{\beta_{k}}{r^{2}}
with
\beta_{k}=kB
and

\displaystyle f_{1}=ik\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}w_{l}\frac{\partial_{r}\breve{\varphi}_{k-l}}{r}+\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}i(k-l)(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{2r^{2}})w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{k-l},\quad f_{2}=\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{k-l}}{r},

with
\breve{\varphi}_{k}
and
\varphi_{k}
satisfy
(\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}-\frac{k^{2}}{r^{2}})\breve{\varphi}_{k}=\frac{e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}w_{k}
and
\varphi_{k}:=r^{\frac{1}{2}}\breve{\varphi}_{k}
. For
\varphi_{k}
, we further deduce

\displaystyle r^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{r}^{2}-\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}})\varphi_{k}=r^{-\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{k},

which yields

(4.1)

\displaystyle(\partial_{r}^{2}-\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}})\varphi_{k}=e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{k}.

Employing (
4.1
), the nonlinear terms
f_{1},f_{2}
can be further expressed as

\displaystyle f_{1}=ik\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}w_{l}\frac{\partial_{r}(r^{-\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{k-l})}{r}+\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}i(k-l)(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{2r^{2}})\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}},\quad f_{2}=\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}.

The following is the main result of this paper:

Theorem 4.1
.

For any
|B|\geq 1
, there exist constants
c_{0},C>0
independent of
B
so that if the initial data satisfies

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w_{k}(0)}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+|B|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w_{0}(0)}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\leq c_{0}|B|^{\frac{1}{3}},

then the solution to the nonlinear vorticity formulation (
1.34
) exists globally in time, and there exists a constant
C>0
independent of
B
such that

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}E_{k}\leq C(\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w_{k}(0)}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+|B|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w_{0}(0)}{r}\|_{L^{2}})\leq Cc_{0}|B|^{\frac{1}{3}}.

4.1.
Interactions between zero frequency and non-zero frequency

Now we establish the estimate involving the interaction between zero frequency and other frequency. Let’s start with the terms including
w_{0}\varphi_{k}
.

Lemma 4.2
.

For any
k\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0\}
, it holds that

\displaystyle|k|\Big(

\displaystyle\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)\lesssim|B|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|k|^{-\frac{5}{6}}E_{0}E_{k}.

Proof.

Applying Hölder’s inequalities for the nonlinear terms in
L^{2}L^{2}
norm, we obtain

\displaystyle|k|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|k|\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}r^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{k}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}\Big).

Combining above inequalities with (
4.1
) and the calculation Lemma
A.5
proved in appendix (with
\beta=-2,-1,0,1
), we derive

\displaystyle|k|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}r^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{k}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|k|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}r^{2}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{k}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}re^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{k}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{k}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}\frac{w_{k}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|k|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}.

Therefore, we deduce

\displaystyle|k|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|k|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq|k|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|B|^{-\frac{1}{6}}E_{0}|kB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}E_{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|B|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|k|^{-\frac{5}{6}}E_{0}E_{k}.

∎

Then we treat
w_{0}\varphi_{k}^{\prime}
terms.

Lemma 4.3
.

For any
k\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0\}
, we have

\displaystyle|k|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}^{\prime}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}^{\prime}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)\lesssim|k|^{-\frac{1}{6}}|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}E_{0}E_{k}.

Proof.

Employing Hölder’s inequalities, we can prove

\displaystyle|k|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}^{\prime}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}^{\prime}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|k|\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k}^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\varphi_{k}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big).

This together with (
4.1
) and Lemma
A.5
yields

\displaystyle|k|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k}^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\varphi_{k}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big)\lesssim\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}.

Hence we deduce

\displaystyle|k|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}^{\prime}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}^{\prime}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq|B|^{-\frac{1}{6}}E_{0}|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}E_{k}=|k|^{-\frac{1}{6}}|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}E_{0}E_{k}.

∎

For quadratic terms containing
w_{k}\partial_{r}(r^{-\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{0})
, we have

Lemma 4.4
.

For any
k\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0\}
, the following inequality holds

\displaystyle|k|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}\partial_{r}(r^{-\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{0})}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}\partial_{r}(r^{-\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{0})}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)\lesssim|k|^{-\frac{1}{6}}|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}E_{0}E_{k}.

Proof.

Recall that
\varphi_{0}
and
w_{0}
satisfy

\displaystyle(\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r})(r^{-\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{0})=\frac{e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}w_{0}.

Construct
G:=\partial_{r}(r^{-\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{0})
, one can check
\partial_{r}(r^{-\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{0})=G
.
Observing the inequality as below,

\displaystyle\|G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\frac{G}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}}=\|\frac{\int_{0}^{r}s^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\frac{s^{2}}{8}}w_{0}ds}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\frac{\int_{0}^{r}s^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\frac{s^{2}}{8}}w_{0}ds}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big\|\frac{(\int_{0}^{r}s^{3}e^{-\frac{s^{2}}{4}}ds)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{r}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}+\Big\|\frac{(\int_{0}^{r}s^{3}e^{-\frac{s^{2}}{4}}ds)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{r^{2}}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big(\|\frac{(\int_{0}^{r}s^{3}e^{-\frac{s^{2}}{4}}ds)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\frac{(\int_{0}^{r}s^{3}e^{-\frac{s^{2}}{4}}ds)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big)\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}.

Together with Hölder’s inequality, we conclude

\displaystyle|k|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}\partial_{r}(r^{-\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{0})}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}\partial_{r}(r^{-\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{0})}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|k|\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}(\|G\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|\frac{G}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}})\lesssim|k|\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}|B|^{-\frac{1}{6}}E_{k}E_{0}=|k|^{-\frac{1}{6}}|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}E_{k}E_{0}.

∎

4.2.
Interactions between non-zero frequency spaces

We now move to control nonlinear terms containing only non-zero frequency.
Firstly, observing the following inequality

(4.2)

\displaystyle|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\leq|lB|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\quad\textrm{for any}\ k,l\in\mathbb{Z},

we obtain

Lemma 4.5
.

For any
k\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0\}
, we have

\displaystyle\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|B|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}|l|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|k-l|^{-\frac{2}{3}}E_{l}E_{k-l}.

Proof.

Utilizing (
4.2
) we obtain

\displaystyle|k-l|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|k-l|\|e^{c|lB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big(\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}r^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{k-l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\Big).

Combining (
4.1
) and the calculation Lemma
A.5
proved in appendix (with
\beta=-4,-2,0,2
), it follows

\displaystyle|k-l|\Big(\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}r^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{k-l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}
