\displaystyle+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|k-l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}r^{2}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{k-l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}re^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{k-l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{k-l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}\frac{w_{k-l}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|k-l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k-l}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}.

Therefore, we deduce

\displaystyle\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}\|e^{c|lB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}|k-l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k-l}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}|lB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}E_{l}|k-l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|(k-l)B|^{-\frac{1}{6}}E_{k-l}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|B|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}|l|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|k-l|^{-\frac{2}{3}}E_{l}E_{k-l}.

This completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

We also have

Lemma 4.6
.

For any
k\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0\}
, it holds

\displaystyle|k|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}^{\prime}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}^{\prime}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}\Big(|l|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|k-l|^{-\frac{1}{6}}E_{l}E_{k-l}+|l|^{-\frac{1}{6}}|k-l|^{-\frac{2}{3}}E_{l}E_{k-l}\Big).

Proof.

Applying (
4.2
) we have

\displaystyle|k-l|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi^{\prime}_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi^{\prime}_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|k-l|\|e^{c|lB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}\Big(\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi^{\prime}_{k-l}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\varphi^{\prime}_{k-l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k-l}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big).

Together with (
4.1
), the calculation Lemma
A.5
proved in appendix (with
\beta
chosen to be
-2
and
0
) this gives

\displaystyle|k-l|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi^{\prime}_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi^{\prime}_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|e^{c|lB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}\Big(\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{k-l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}re^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{k-l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|e^{c|lB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k-l}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|lB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}|l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}E_{l}|(k-l)B|^{-\frac{1}{6}}E_{k-l}=|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|l|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|(k-l)|^{-\frac{1}{6}}E_{l}E_{k-l}.

Also we find

\displaystyle|l|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi^{\prime}_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi^{\prime}_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|l|\|e^{c|lB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big(\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi^{\prime}_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}r^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}_{k-l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\Big).

Employing (
4.1
) and calculation Lemma
A.5
proved in appendix (with
\beta=-2,0
), we obtain

\displaystyle|l|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi^{\prime}_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi^{\prime}_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|l|\|e^{c|lB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}|k-l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{k-l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}re^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{k-l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|l|\|e^{c|lB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{l}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}|k-l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{c|(k-l)B|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k-l}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|lB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}E_{l}|k-l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|(k-l)B|^{-\frac{1}{6}}E_{k-l}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|l|^{-\frac{1}{6}}|k-l|^{-\frac{2}{3}}E_{l}E_{k-l}.

Therefore, with applying the basic inequality
|k|\lesssim|k-l||l|
for
l\neq 0,k
, we conclude

\displaystyle|k|\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}^{\prime}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}^{\prime}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}\Big(|l|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|k-l|^{-\frac{1}{6}}E_{l}E_{k-l}+|l|^{-\frac{1}{6}}|k-l|^{-\frac{2}{3}}E_{l}E_{k-l}\Big).

This completes the proof of this lemma.
∎

Finally, we prove the following bound for terms involving
w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}
.

Lemma 4.7
.

For nonlinear terms involving
w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}
(
l\neq 0
), we have the bounds as below

\displaystyle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|l|\|w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|l|\|\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\lesssim|B|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|l|^{-1}E_{l}E_{-l}.

Proof.

Recall (
4.1
) and for
\varphi_{k},\breve{\varphi}_{k}
we have
breve{\varphi}_{k}=\frac{\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, and

\displaystyle(\partial_{r}^{2}-\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}})\varphi_{k}=e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{k}.

Employing Hölder’s inequalities, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|l|\|w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|l|\|\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|l|\|\frac{w_{l}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}(\|\frac{\varphi_{-l}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|\frac{\varphi_{-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}),

Combining with (
4.1
) and Lemma
A.5
, we derive

\displaystyle|l|\Big(\|\frac{\varphi_{-l}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|\frac{\varphi_{-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\|re^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{-l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8}}w_{-l}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w_{-l}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}.

Hence we conclude

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}ilw_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}\|\frac{w_{l}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}|l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{w_{-l}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|lB|^{-\frac{1}{3}}E_{l}|lB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}E_{-l}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|B|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|l|^{-1}E_{l}E_{-l}.

∎

4.3.
Proof of Theorem
4.1

With above estimates at hand, we are now ready to prove the main theorem of this paper.

Proof of Theorem
4.1
.

Recall that in Proposition
3.8
and Proposition
3.9
we have derived the following space-time estimates

\displaystyle\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}},

and

\displaystyle|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}X}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}\|_{L^{2}X}

\displaystyle+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\partial_{r}w_{k}\|_{L^{2}X}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}(\frac{|k|}{r}+r)w_{k}\|_{L^{2}X}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}X}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{f_{2}}{r}\|_{L^{2}X})+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}X}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X},

where
X=L^{2}(\frac{1}{r^{2}})
is the weighted
L^{2}
space we construct, and
f_{1},f_{2}
are nonlinear terms given by

\displaystyle f_{1}=ik\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}w_{l}\frac{\partial_{r}(r^{-\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{k-l})}{r}+\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}i(k-l)(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{2r^{2}})\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}},\quad f_{2}=\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}.

Using the calculation Lemma
A.3
proved in appendix (with
\alpha=\frac{3}{2}
), we obtain

\displaystyle|k|\|\frac{w_{k}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\lesssim|k|\|\frac{w_{k}^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w_{k}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+|k|\|\frac{w_{k}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|\frac{w_{k}^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+k^{2}\|\frac{w_{k}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\lesssim\|\frac{w_{0}^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|\frac{w_{0}^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

This implies

\displaystyle|k|\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}^{2}\lesssim\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+k^{2}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}^{2}\lesssim\|\frac{w_{0}^{\prime}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

Therefore, following Proposition
3.8
, Proposition
3.9
, and Proposition
3.10
, we deduce

\displaystyle E_{k}=

\displaystyle\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}w_{k}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+|k|\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+|k|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}\Big)+|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}X}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{f_{2}}{r}\|_{L^{2}X}

\displaystyle+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}X}+\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X},\quad|k|\geq 1,

\displaystyle E_{0}=

\displaystyle|B|^{\frac{1}{6}}(\|\frac{w_{0}}{r}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|\frac{w_{0}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|w_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|B|^{\frac{1}{6}}(\|\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}ilw_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}il\frac{w_{l}\breve{\varphi}_{-l}}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\frac{w_{0}(0)}{r}\|_{L^{2}}),\quad k=0.

To bound the terms on the right hand side, we utilize the following inequality

(4.3)

\displaystyle|k|\lesssim|k-l||l|\quad\mbox{if}\ k\neq 0,l.

With (
4.3
), Lemma
4.2
, Lemma
4.3
, Lemma
4.4
, Lemma
4.5
and Lemma
4.6
, we have that the first term
|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}
can be controlled via

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\Big(|k|\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}\frac{w_{l}\partial_{r}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+|k|\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+|k|\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\partial_{r}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+|k|\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+|k|\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}\partial_{r}(r^{-\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{0})}{r}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}ik\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}ik\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}(|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|k|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}E_{l}E_{k-l}+|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|k|^{-\frac{1}{6}}E_{0}E_{k})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|B|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|k|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}E_{l}E_{k-l}.

For the estimate of
f_{2}
, we appeal to (
4.3
), Lemma
4.2
and Lemma
4.5
again. And we obtain

\displaystyle\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{f_{2}}{r}\|_{L^{2}X}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}X}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}(|B|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}|l|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|k-l|^{-\frac{2}{3}}E_{l}E_{k-l}+|B|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|k|^{-\frac{5}{6}}E_{0}E_{k})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|k|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}E_{l}E_{k-l}.

It remains to handle the item
\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}X}
. Applying (
4.3
), Lemma
4.2
, Lemma
4.3
, Lemma
4.4
, Lemma
4.5
and Lemma
4.6
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}X}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}ik\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}^{\prime}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}ik\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+|k|\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\partial_{r}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+|k|\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+|k|\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{w_{k}\partial_{r}(r^{-\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{0})}{r^{2}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}i(k-l)\frac{w_{l}\varphi_{k-l}}{r^{\frac{7}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}ik\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}ik\frac{w_{0}\varphi_{k}}{r^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|k|^{-\frac{1}{6}}(\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0,k\}}E_{l}E_{k-l}+E_{0}E_{k})\lesssim|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|k|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}E_{l}E_{k-l}.

Finally, we arrive at

\displaystyle E_{k}\lesssim

\displaystyle|kB|^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}X}+\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}\frac{f_{2}}{r}\|_{L^{2}X}

\displaystyle+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c|kB|^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}X}+\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|k|^{-\frac{1}{6}}|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}E_{l}E_{k-l}+\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X},

In addition, using Lemma
4.7
, we also find

\displaystyle E_{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}E_{l}E_{-l}+|B|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w_{0}(0)}{r}\|_{L^{2}}.

With the bootstrap assumption

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}E_{k}\leq c_{0}B^{\frac{1}{3}},

we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}E_{k}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big[|B|^{-\frac{1}{3}}(\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}E_{k})^{2}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{X}+|B|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w_{0}(0)}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big[c_{0}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}E_{k}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w_{k}(0)}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+|B|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w_{0}(0)}{r}\|_{L^{2}}\Big].

Choose
c_{0}\leq\frac{1}{2}C^{-1}
, we hence prove

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}E_{k}\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}\|w_{k}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|kB|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w_{k}(0)}{r}\|_{L^{2}}+|B|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{w_{0}(0)}{r}\|_{L^{2}}.

The above completes the proof of Theorem
4.1
.
∎

4.4.
Proof of Theorem
1.8

As a corollary of the above main result, we also obtain
an improved transition threshold for the original equations (
1.2
). Let
\tilde{\omega}:=\frac{w-2A_{1}t}{t}
. We have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|\tilde{\omega}(t,x)|dx=

\displaystyle\nu t\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|\frac{w-2A_{1}t}{t}|d\xi=\nu\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|w(\tau,\xi)-2A_{1}e^{\tau}|d\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nu\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}r|w(\tau,r,\theta)-2A_{1}e^{\tau}|drd\theta.

Note that
w(\tau,\xi)=w(\tau,r,\theta)=\sum\limits_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\omega_{k}(\tau,r)e^{ik\theta}
. And recall the following estimate for
\tau\geq 0
in Theorem
1.5

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}(\tau)=|\frac{A_{2}}{\nu}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{\omega_{0}(\tau)-2A_{1}e^{\tau}}{r}\|_{\mathcal{M}}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}(\|\omega_{k}(\tau)\|_{\mathcal{M}}+|k|^{\frac{1}{6}}|\frac{A_{2}}{\nu}|^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\frac{\omega_{k}(\tau)}{r}\|_{\mathcal{M}})\leq Cc_{0}|\frac{A_{2}}{\nu}|^{\frac{1}{3}}.

We thus obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|\tilde{\omega}(t,x)|dx=

\displaystyle\nu\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}r|w(\tau,r,\theta)-2A_{1}e^{\tau}|drd\theta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\pi\nu\int_{0}^{\infty}r(|\omega_{0}(\tau)-2A_{1}e^{\tau}|+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}|\omega_{k}|)dr

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\pi\nu(\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{3}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4}}dr)^{\frac{1}{2}}(\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{-1}e^{\frac{r^{2}}{4}}|\omega_{0}(\tau)-2A_{1}e^{\tau}|^{2}dr)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+2\pi\nu\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}(\int_{0}^{\infty}re^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4}}dr)^{\frac{1}{2}}(\int_{0}^{\infty}re^{\frac{r^{2}}{4}}|\omega_{k}(\tau)|^{2}dr)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\pi\nu(\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{3}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4}}dr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{\omega_{0}(\tau)-2A_{1}e^{\tau}}{r}\|_{\mathcal{M}}

\displaystyle+2\pi\nu(\int_{0}^{\infty}re^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4}}dr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}/\{0\}}\|\omega_{k}(\tau)\|_{\mathcal{M}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\nu\mathcal{E}(\tau)\leq Cc_{0}|\frac{A_{2}}{\nu}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\nu=Cc_{0}|A_{2}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\nu^{\frac{2}{3}}.

Hence, for initial data at
t=1
, if the initial perturbation satisfies

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|\tilde{\omega}(1,x)|dx\lesssim\nu\mathcal{E}(0)\leq Cc_{0}|A_{2}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\nu^{\frac{2}{3}},

then we proof

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|\tilde{\omega}(t,x)|dx\lesssim\nu\mathcal{E}(\tau)\lesssim\nu\mathcal{E}(0)\leq Cc_{0}|A_{2}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\nu^{\frac{2}{3}},\quad\ for\ all\ t\geq 1.

This is corresponding to the improved transition threshold stated in the Theorem
1.8
.

Appendix A
Calculation lemmas

Lemma A.1
.

For any
A,B\in\mathbb{R}
, the below ODE admits a global solution (with variable
r\in(0,\infty)
)

(A.1)

\displaystyle\frac{g^{\prime\prime}}{g}+\frac{A}{r}\frac{g^{\prime}}{g}=\frac{B}{r^{2}}.

Proof.

Let
G:=\frac{g^{\prime}}{g}
. By noting that

\displaystyle G^{\prime}=\frac{g^{\prime\prime}g-g^{\prime 2}}{g^{2}}=\frac{g^{\prime\prime}}{g}-G^{2},

we can transfer the above equation (
A.1
) to

\displaystyle G^{\prime}+G^{2}+\frac{A}{r}G=\frac{B}{r^{2}}.

Denote
F=G+\frac{A}{2r}
, we deduce

F^{\prime}+F^{2}=\frac{C}{r^{2}}

with
C=\frac{A^{2}}{4}+\frac{A}{2}+B
.
Dividing both sides by
F^{2}
and via introducing
H=\frac{1}{F}
, one obtains

-H^{\prime}+1=C(\frac{H}{r})^{2},

Let
K=\frac{H}{r}
, we then have

-(rK^{\prime}+K)+1=CK^{2},

or equivalently

K^{\prime}=\frac{-CK^{2}-K+1}{r}.

The above separable equation can be solved via

\int\frac{dK}{-CK^{2}-K+1}=\log r.

Therefore, we deduce

g(r,A,B)=C_{0}\exp(\int_{0}^{r}G(s)ds)=C_{0}\exp(\int_{0}^{r}\frac{1}{sK(s)}-\frac{A}{2s}ds),

∎

Lemma A.2
.

For any
w\in L^{2}(\Gamma),w|_{r\in\partial\Gamma}=0
, it holds

\displaystyle\|w\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\leq 2\|w\|_{L^{2}}\|w^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}.

Proof.

Since
w|_{r\in\partial\Gamma}=0
, we have

\displaystyle|w(r)|^{2}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{r}\partial_{s}(|w(s)|^{2})ds=\int_{0}^{r}(w^{\prime}(s)\overline{w}(s)+w(s)\overline{w}^{\prime}(s))ds\leq 2\|w\|_{L^{2}}\|w^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}},

∎

Lemma A.3
.

For any
w\in L^{2}(\Gamma)
and
\alpha\in\mathbb{R}
, it holds

\displaystyle\|\frac{w}{r^{\alpha}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\lesssim\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r^{\alpha+\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\frac{w}{r^{\alpha+\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Proof.

Choose
r_{1}\in(0,1)
such that
|\frac{|w(r_{1})|^{2}}{r_{1}^{2\alpha}}|\leq\|\frac{w}{r^{\alpha}}\|_{L^{2}(0,1)}^{2}\leq\|\frac{w}{r^{\alpha+\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}(0,1)}^{2}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{|w(r)|^{2}}{r^{2\alpha}}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{r_{1}}^{r}\partial_{s}(\frac{|w(s)|^{2}}{s^{\alpha}})ds+\|\frac{w}{r^{\alpha+\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}(0,1)}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{r_{1}}^{r}\frac{w^{\prime}(s)\overline{w}(s)+w(s)\overline{w}^{\prime}(s)}{s^{2\alpha}}-2\alpha\frac{|w(s)|^{2}}{s^{2\alpha+1}}ds+\|\frac{w}{r^{\alpha+\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}(0,1)}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\frac{w^{\prime}}{r^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\frac{w}{r^{\alpha+\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\frac{w}{r^{\alpha+\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

This completes the proof of this lemma.
∎

Lemma A.4
.

Let
w=\varphi^{\prime\prime}-\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}\varphi\in L^{2}(\Gamma)
with
w|_{r\in\partial\Gamma}=0,\varphi|_{r\in\partial\Gamma}=0
. Then for any
|k|\geq 1
and
\beta\in\mathbb{R}
it holds

\displaystyle\Re\langle-w,r^{\beta}\varphi\rangle\gtrsim\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+k^{2}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Proof.

For any
C^{2}
real function
g
with variable
r
, we have the following identity:

\displaystyle-\varphi^{\prime\prime}=-g^{-1}\partial_{r}(r^{\beta}g^{2}\partial_{r}(r^{-\beta}g^{-1}\varphi))+\frac{\beta}{r}\varphi^{\prime}-g^{-1}[g^{\prime\prime}-(\frac{\beta}{r}g)^{\prime}]\varphi.

Via integration by parts we obtain

\displaystyle\langle-w,r^{\beta}\varphi\rangle=

\displaystyle\langle-g^{-1}\partial_{r}(r^{\beta}g^{2}\partial_{r}(r^{-\beta}g^{-1}\varphi))+\frac{\beta}{r}\varphi^{\prime}-g^{-1}[g^{\prime\prime}-(\frac{\beta}{r}g)^{\prime}]\varphi+\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}\varphi,\frac{\varphi}{r^{\beta}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|r^{-\frac{\beta}{2}}g\partial_{r}(r^{\beta}g^{-1}\varphi)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\beta\langle\varphi^{\prime},r^{\beta-1}\varphi\rangle-\langle g^{-1}[g^{\prime\prime}-(\frac{\beta}{r}g)^{\prime}]\varphi,r^{\beta}\varphi\rangle

\displaystyle+(k^{2}-\frac{1}{4})\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

This together with

(A.2)

\displaystyle\Re\langle\varphi^{\prime},r^{\beta-1}\varphi\rangle=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma}r^{\beta-1}d|\varphi|^{2}=-\frac{\beta-1}{2}\int_{\Gamma}r^{\beta-2}|\varphi|^{2}dr,

gives

\displaystyle\Re\langle-w,r^{\beta}\varphi\rangle=

\displaystyle\|r^{-\frac{\beta}{2}}g\partial_{r}(r^{\beta}g^{-1}\varphi)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\langle g^{-1}(g^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\beta}{r}g^{\prime})\varphi,r^{\beta}\varphi\rangle

\displaystyle+(k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\beta(\beta+1)}{2})\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Choosing
g=g(r,A,B)
as in Lemma
A.1
with
A=-\beta,B=\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\beta(\beta+1)}{2}
, then
\frac{g^{\prime\prime}}{g}-\frac{\beta}{r}\frac{g^{\prime}}{g}=\frac{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\beta(\beta+1)}{2}}{r^{2}}
. Hence we deduce

(A.3)

\displaystyle\Re\langle-w,r^{\beta}\varphi\rangle=\|r^{-\frac{\beta}{2}}g\partial_{r}(r^{\beta}g^{-1}\varphi)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+k^{2}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\geq k^{2}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Employing integration by parts and (
A.2
), we also have

\displaystyle\Re\langle-w,r^{\beta}\varphi\rangle=

\displaystyle\langle-\varphi^{\prime\prime}+\frac{k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}}{r^{2}}\varphi,r^{\beta}\varphi\rangle\

(A.4)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\beta\Re\langle\varphi^{\prime},r^{\beta}\varphi\rangle+(k^{2}-\frac{1}{4})\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+(k^{2}-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\beta(\beta-1)}{2})\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Combining (
A
) and (
A.3
), we now conclude

(A.5)

\displaystyle\Re\langle-w,r^{\beta}\varphi\rangle\gtrsim\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+k^{2}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

∎

Lemma A.5
.

Under the same conditions of Lemma
A.4
, we have

\displaystyle\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}\varphi^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}}+|k|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|r^{\frac{\beta+1}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+|k|\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}+|k|^{\frac{3}{2}}\|r^{\frac{\beta-1}{2}}\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}}+k^{2}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}w\|_{L^{2}}.

Proof.

Recall in Lemma
A.4
, we have the following inequality

\displaystyle\Re\langle-w,r^{\beta}\varphi\rangle\gtrsim\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+k^{2}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Combining with

\displaystyle\Re\langle-w,r^{\beta}\varphi\rangle\leq\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}w\|_{L^{2}}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}\leq|k|^{-1}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}w\|_{L^{2}}(\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}+|k|\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}})

and

\displaystyle\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+k^{2}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\gtrsim(\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}+|k|\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}})^{2},

we obtain

(A.6)

\displaystyle|k|\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}+k^{2}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}w\|_{L^{2}}.

Employing Lemma
A.3
(with
\alpha=-\frac{\beta-1}{2}
) and (
A.6
) we get

\displaystyle\|r^{\frac{\beta-1}{2}}\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\lesssim\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}+\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim|k|^{-3}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}w\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

To derive the bound for
\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}\varphi^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}}
, we apply the following equality

\displaystyle\Re\langle w,r^{\beta+2}\varphi^{\prime\prime}\rangle=\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}\varphi^{\prime\prime}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}-(k^{2}-\frac{1}{4})\Re\langle\varphi,r^{\beta+2}\varphi^{\prime\prime}\rangle.

By Hölder’s inequality we have

\displaystyle\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}\varphi^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}\varphi^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}w\|_{L^{2}}+k^{2}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}\varphi^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}},

which yields

(A.7)

\displaystyle\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}\varphi^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}w\|_{L^{2}}+k^{2}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}w\|_{L^{2}}.

Here in the second inequality we appeal to (
A.6
).

Finally, we utilize Lemma
A.3
again with
\alpha=-\frac{\beta+1}{2}
. Together with (
A.6
) and (
A.7
), we deduce

\displaystyle\|r^{\frac{\beta+1}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\lesssim\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}\varphi^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}+\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim|k|^{-2}\|r^{\frac{\beta}{2}+1}w\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

∎
